# Google Search Console: Master Daily Indexing Blueprint & URL Schedule
**Domain:** [https://hentaikage.cc](https://hentaikage.cc)  
**Total Catalog:** 273 Published Series | 653 Published Episodes | Core Hubs & Taxonomies  
**Target Submission Rate:** 15–20 URLs per day (Google Search Console safe daily inspection quota)

---

## 🚀 Quick Execution Protocol (How to Submit in 2 Minutes/Day)

1. Open your **Google Search Console** dashboard for `https://hentaikage.cc/`.
2. Look at the top search bar: `Inspect any URL in "https://hentaikage.cc/"`.
3. Copy the URL from today's batch.
4. Paste it into the search bar and press **Enter**.
5. Click **"TEST LIVE URL"** (takes ~10-15 seconds).
6. Click **"REQUEST INDEXING"**.
7. Repeat for all URLs in that day's batch.

> [!TIP]
> **Daily Quota Limit**: Google Search Console allows approximately **10 to 20 manual indexing requests per day**. If you see a message saying *"Quota exceeded for today, please try again tomorrow"*, stop for the day and resume with the next batch the following day.
